### [1] [Offering Microsecond-Scale Cross-VM Core Elasticity on Colocated Lightweight Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12633)
*Yibo Yan,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HyperFlux是一种基于商品化KVM的超轻量级VM底层系统，使VM的并行宽度（物理核心数）在运行时具有弹性，可在仅13μs内跨VM迁移核心，内存占用仅3.2MB，冷启动仅需1.37ms，同时支持多核并行，在高负载混杂部署下可将高优先级VM的尾延迟降低最多10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless平台通常将多样化的负载共置在快速启动、内存精简的VM中以提高部署密度。但为了在流量突发时保护尾延迟，往往需要为每个VM过度配置资源，这会损害部署密度。现有VM底层系统无法在微秒级时间尺度上动态调整物理核心分配：传统VM通过毫秒级vCPU热插拔调整核心，Firecracker在启动时固定核心数，而超轻量VM则完全放弃多核执行。因此亟需一种能在微秒级弹性调整VM并行宽度的底层系统。

Method: HyperFlux基于商品化KVM构建超轻量级VM底层系统，使VM的并行宽度（支撑它的物理核心数）在运行时具有弹性。其核心创新在于能够在微秒级时间尺度（13μs）内在VM之间迁移物理核心，即使是从繁忙的捐赠者VM中强制回收核心也能达到该速度，远快于vCPU热插拔方案。

Result: HyperFlux可以在仅13μs内跨VM移动核心（即使从繁忙的捐赠者强制回收），比vCPU热插拔快数个数量级。VM内存占用仅3.2MB，冷启动时间为1.37ms，与最快的超轻量级VM相当，同时独特地支持多核并行。在混杂部署下，与Firecracker和Cloud Hypervisor的静态核心共享相比，可将高优先级VM的尾延迟在高负载下降低最多10倍；与cgroup和vCPU热插拔方案相比，在变化的负载突发下能提供更低且更稳定的尾延迟。

Conclusion: HyperFlux证明可以构建一种兼具超轻量级VM的快速启动与低内存占用、同时支持弹性多核并行的VM底层系统，使得Serverless基础设施能够在保持高部署密度的同时有效保护延迟敏感型VM的尾延迟，为Serverless共置场景提供了更优的基础设施方案。

Abstract: Serverless platforms commonly colocate many diverse workloads, each in a fast-booting, memory-lean virtual machine (VM), to improve deployment density. Overprovisioning each VM for its peak protects tail latency during traffic bursts but hurts density; maintaining high density while effectively protecting tail latency requires the infrastructure to be able to shift physical cores, at a microsecond timescale, to whichever latency-sensitive VM is bursting and reclaim them as the burst subsides. No VM substrate delivers this: conventional VMs resize a guest's cores only through a millisecond-scale vCPU hot-plug path, Firecracker fixes a VM's core count at boot, and the ultralight VMs that boot fastest drop multicore execution entirely.
  We present HyperFlux, a commodity-KVM ultralight VM substrate that makes a VM's parallelism width (the number of physical cores backing it) elastic at runtime. We show that HyperFlux can move a core across VMs in merely 13$μ$s, even when forcibly reclaiming it from a busy donor, orders of magnitude faster than vCPU hot-plug. A HyperFlux VM incurs only a 3.2MB memory footprint and can cold-boot in 1.37ms, on par with the fastest-booting ultralight VMs, while uniquely supporting multicore parallelism. Under colocation, it can reduce high-priority VMs' tail latency by up to 10x under high load compared to static core-sharing with Firecracker and Cloud Hypervisor, and deliver a lower and more stable tail latency compared to using cgroup and vCPU hot-plug under changing load bursts.

</details>


### [2] [InFactPlanner: Planning Sustainable Geo-Distributed LLM Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12915)
*Nicoletta Tsiopani,Moysis Symeonides,George Pallis,Marios D. Dikaiakos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出InFactPlanner，一个基于轨迹驱动的决策支持框架，用于大型语言模型推理的可持续AI数据中心部署的假设分析，支持单站点和地理分布式站点的能耗、碳排放、水耗、延迟和服务器利用率评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理的快速增长使得可持续性关注从一次性训练转向持续服务。然而，运营商在建设大规模基础设施之前往往需要比较不同的部署方案，直接测量成本高、速度慢，有时甚至不可行，因此亟需一个能够在建设前进行部署方案比较的决策支持工具。

Method: InFactPlanner结合查询轨迹、硬件模型配置、候选站点配置、PUE/WUE参数、可再生能源生成模型和时变电网碳强度，估计功耗、能量、碳排放、水耗、延迟和服务器利用率。框架将底层服务效应抽象为可配置的硬件-模型配置文件，支持站点选择、容量放置、硬件、模型、可再生能源整合和路由选择的快速比较。

Result: 通过复现参考LLM推理能量估计验证了能量核算流程，偏差小于10%；在多个数据中心和服务器数量上评估了可扩展性；展示了硬件选择、可再生能源放置、地理部署和碳感知路由等场景驱动决策分析。结果表明可持续性最优选择可能不同于延迟最优选择，部署的碳价值高度依赖于当地电网结构。

Conclusion: InFactPlanner能够为可持续AI数据中心部署提供有效的假设分析支持，揭示了可持续性目标与延迟目标之间可能存在冲突，且部署决策的碳价值受电网碳强度特征显著影响，为运营商在建设前做出更优的可持续部署决策提供了量化工具。

Abstract: The rapid growth of LLM inference is shifting sustainability concerns from one-time training to continuous serving, where infrastructure decisions shape energy use, carbon emissions, water consumption, and service quality. Yet operators often need to compare deployment alternatives before large-scale infrastructure is built, making direct measurement costly, slow, and sometimes infeasible. We present InFactPlanner, a trace-driven decision-support framework for what-if analysis of sustainable AI data center deployment for LLM inference across single and geo-distributed sites. InFactPlanner combines query traces, hardware-model profiles, candidate site configurations, PUE/WUE parameters, renewable generation models, and time-varying grid carbon intensity to estimate power, energy, carbon emissions, water use, latency, and server utilization. The framework abstracts low-level serving effects into configurable hardware-model profiles, enabling rapid comparison of site selection, capacity placement, hardware, model, renewable integration, and routing choices. We validate the energy accounting pipeline by reproducing reference LLM inference energy estimates with less than 10% deviation, evaluate scalability across multiple data centers and server counts, and demonstrate scenario-driven decision analyses for hardware selection, renewable placement, geographic deployment, and carbon-aware routing. Our results show that sustainability-optimal choices can differ from latency-optimal ones, and that the carbon value of deployment depends strongly on the local grid mix.

</details>


### [3] [Efficient Randomized LL/SC that Preserves History Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12946)
*Dante Bencivenga,Homa Habashi,Philipp Woelfel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究在n个进程的系统中实现m个线性化LL/SC对象的问题，通过引入随机化和FADD（以及CAS和寄存器），在弱自适应对手模型下将空间复杂度从已知的Ω(n²τ+m)降低到O(nτ+m)，当m=O(1)时匹配下界；同时该实现支持静止历史无关（QHI）性质，可应用于动态哈希算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LL/SC对象是并发编程中的重要原语，但现有最优确定性算法需要Ω(n²τ+m)个基本对象，空间开销大；且硬件中不直接提供LL/SC，需要软件实现。此前没有可比较的随机化算法，也没有具有类似性质的等待自由或高效无锁软件实现，无法在现有硬件上运行依赖LL/SC的算法（如STOC 2025上的QHI动态哈希算法）。

Method: 利用随机化和FADD指令（结合CAS和寄存器），针对弱自适应对手模型设计LL/SC对象的软件实现，将空间复杂度从Ω(n²τ+m)降至O(nτ+m)。该实现满足静止历史无关（QHI）性质，即无挂起操作时内部存储状态由m个LL/SC对象的值唯一确定。

Result: 获得O(nτ+m)的空间下界，当m=O(1)时与使用CAS和寄存器的算法下界匹配。该实现可直接支持QHI动态哈希算法（STOC 2025），在m=Ω(n)的合理假设下，无需在步复杂度和空间复杂度上渐进增加即可在现有硬件上运行。

Conclusion: 本文首次展示了可以在常见硬件上以常数期望步复杂度实现LL/SC对象，且空间复杂度达到接近最优；同时证明了依赖LL/SC的QHI算法（如动态哈希）可以在不损失渐近效率的情况下部署到实际硬件上，填补了理论算法与实际硬件之间的空白。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of implementing $m$ linearizable LL/SC objects with constant expected step complexity in a system of $n$ processes, using bounded base objects commonly available in hardware. Assuming that each process may have at most $τ$ outstanding LL operations, the best known deterministic algorithm requires $Ω(n^2τ+ m)$ base objects (CAS and registers) [Blelloch and Wei, DISC 2020]. Previously, no comparable randomized algorithm was known.
  By employing randomization and FADD in addition to CAS and registers, we obtain a space bound of $O(nτ+m)$ against the weak adaptive adversary. For $m=O(1)$ this matches a lower bound for algorithms using CAS and registers [Aghazadeh and Woelfel, PODC 2015].
  In addition, our object can be employed by quiescently history-independent (QHI) algorithms: Whenever no operation on the object is pending and no process has an outstanding LL operation, its internal memory state is uniquely determined by the values of the $m$ LL/SC objects. An important application is a QHI dynamic hashing algorithm presented at STOC 2025, which uses $Θ(m)$ hardware LL/SC objects to maintain a hash table of size $m$ [Attiya, Bender, Farach-Colton, Oshman, and Schiller, STOC 2025]. But LL/SC is not available in hardware, and prior to our work no wait-free or efficiently lock-free software implementation of LL/SC with similar properties was known. Our work demonstrates that one can actually implement the hashing algorithm on available hardware, without an asymptotic increase in step and space complexity, under the reasonable assumption that $m=Ω(n)$.

</details>


### [4] [TEMPO: Makespan-Aware Expert-Parallel Load Balancing Across Memory- and Compute-Bound Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13057)
*Jie Li,Chenxin Jia,Jinliang Shen,Cunzhuang Liu,Ruiyi Ding,Jianwen Xian,Kang He,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文发现专家并行MoE服务中每层同步受限于最慢GPU，现有调度假设专家时间与token数或激活专家数线性相关，但实测显示存在两个区间：低于约156-168个token时HBM权重流主导（成本与激活副本挂钩），高于该值时分组GEMM将token四舍五入到128-tile的M块导致专家拆分产生填充计算。作者提出max-affine模型t=max(a+bG, c+βN)刻画两个区间，将逐批次调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题（NP-hard），并提出makespan感知调度器sys，在8-GPU测试台上优于基线1%-15.5%，在Qwen3-235B上提升4-6%吞吐量并降低p99延迟约15.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EP MoE调度器的核心假设（专家时间与token数或激活专家数线性相关）与真实GPU行为不符，导致调度代理（proxy）与实际块时间存在1.4-1.6倍偏差，且代理的最优选择会随工作区间翻转，需要更精确的调度模型和算法。

Method: 通过两代数据中心GPU的实测揭示双区间行为，提出max-affine执行时间模型；将逐批次调度形式化为固定费用makespan问题并分析其复杂度；设计makespan感知的调度器sys，以毫秒级求解，与SGLang集成并采用进程外运行、将调度与计数收集融合为单图内核。

Result: 在8-GPU Testbed A微基准上，sys在所有场景下与最佳固定基线差距在1%以内，混合区间场景最高提升15.5%。端到端Testbed B上，Qwen3-235B（处于赢区）吞吐量提升4-6%，p99延迟降低约15.6%；DeepSeek-V3（通信主导，处于赢区外）仅产生机制开销。

Conclusion: 核心主张是提供一个相位图（phase diagram）而非普适胜利：该图能在部署前预测系统处于赢区还是输区，从而决定sys是否适用。两个端到端模型的结果正是该预测能力的验证。

Abstract: In expert-parallel (EP) MoE serving, every layer synchronizes at the slowest GPU. Dispatchers balance token counts (EPLB, LPLB, UltraEP) or activated-expert counts (METRO), assuming expert time is linear in one. Measurements on two datacenter GPU generations show it is neither: below $\nstar\!\approx\!156$--$168$ tokens, HBM weight streaming dominates---cost attaches to \emph{activated replicas}, not tokens; above it, grouped GEMM rounds tokens to 128-tile $M$-tiles, so \emph{splitting} an expert adds padded compute. A max-affine profile $t=\max(a+bG,\,c+βN)$ captures both regimes. Realistic decode batches hold hot experts in the linear regime and cold in the flat \emph{simultaneously}; recorded batches show proxy dispatches differ by $1.4$--$1.6\times$ in modeled block time (p95 up to $1.7\times$), and \emph{which} proxy wins flips with the regime. We formalize per-batch dispatch as a fixed-charge makespan problem---NP-hard on two fully replicated GPUs, polynomial in degenerate limits---and present \sys{}, a makespan-aware dispatcher solving it in milliseconds off the critical path; its SGLang integration runs out-of-process and fuses dispatch with count collection into one in-graph kernel. Anchored by an 8-GPU Testbed~A microbenchmark, \sys{} stays within 1\% of the best fixed baseline everywhere and wins by up to $15.5\%$ where regimes mix. End-to-end on Testbed~B, Qwen3-235B (inside the win region) gains $4$--$6\%$ throughput and cuts p99 latency by ${\sim}15.6\%$; DeepSeek-V3 (outside, communication-dominated) shows only mechanism cost. A phase diagram, not a universal win, is the claim: it predicts both outcomes before deployment.

</details>


### [5] [Meshlib: In-Process Policy Enforcement for Sidecar-less Service Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13107)
*Habib Mostafaei,Tom van Liempd*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Meshlib，一个基于Cilium控制面扩展的无Sidecar服务网格方案，通过应用内嵌库强制Layer-7策略，利用eBPF数据面处理传输层身份和路由，消除Sidecar延迟开销，同时保持服务网格安全语义，在TrainTicket基准上实现最低端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务网格依赖每Pod的Sidecar代理，在每次请求上执行冗余的应用层解析，引入显著的延迟和资源开销。在不牺牲安全保证的前提下消除Sidecar仍然是一个核心挑战。

Method: Meshlib作为Cilium控制面扩展，包含一个非侵入式的应用绑定库，在应用进程内强制Layer-7策略，同时将传输层身份和路由委托给Cilium的eBPF数据面。职责分离消除了Sidecar引入的延迟，保持了服务网格的安全语义，且架构保持与未修改服务的完全互操作，支持增量部署。

Result: 在TrainTicket基准上，针对37个服务强制126条安全策略，与Istio、Linkerd和未修改的Cilium进行比较，Meshlib实现了所有配置中最低的端到端延迟，且资源开销相当。

Conclusion: Meshlib通过将应用层策略执行与应用进程绑定、传输层功能下放至eBPF数据面，成功在无Sidecar架构下兼顾低延迟、安全语义保持和增量采用的灵活性，为服务网格提供了一种轻量级替代方案。

Abstract: Service meshes facilitate service-to-service communication and enforce security policies in microservice architectures. However, they often depend on per-pod sidecar proxies, which introduce significant latency and resource overhead due to redundant application-layer parsing on every request. Eliminating sidecars without compromising security guarantees remains a central challenge. To address this, we introduce Meshlib, a sidecar-less service mesh extension built on Cilium as a control-plane extension. Meshlib incorporates a non-intrusive application-bound library that enforces Layer-7 policies within the application process, while delegating transport-level identity and routing to Cilium's eBPF-based data plane. This separation of responsibilities removes sidecar-induced latency and maintains the security semantics of the service mesh. The architecture remains fully interoperable with unmodified services, enabling incremental adoption within existing deployments. We evaluate Meshlib against Istio, Linkerd, and unmodified Cilium on the TrainTicket benchmark, enforcing 126 security policies across 37 services and show that it achieves the lowest end-to-end latency of all evaluated configurations with comparable resource overhead.

</details>


### [6] [Validation-Centric AI-Assisted GPU Porting of a 250,000+ Line Legacy Weather Simulation Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13122)
*Tetsuya Hoshino,Masaya Kato,Kazuhisa Tsuboki,Daichi Mukunoki,Takahiro Katagiri,Toshihiro Hanawa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过CReSS（25万行Fortran气象模拟代码）案例，提出了一种以验证为中心的AI辅助GPU移植工作流，成功生成162个目标内核的GPU实现，实现5.1倍应用级加速，并检测出5个内核存在由浮点与内建函数差异导致的数值不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型遗留科学应用不仅是旧代码，更是经过长期积累的科学资产，GPU移植必须保留其科学有效性。现有AI辅助代码生成工具虽然能加速GPU移植，但缺乏针对数值验证的系统性设计。

Method: 采用AI代理从遗留Fortran代码中提取OpenMP区域，生成基于物理有效模拟状态的dump内核基准，应用OpenACC变换，并通过逐元素比较与应用程序级验证双重机制确认数值正确性。

Result: 对真实台风模拟案例，成功为162个目标内核生成经过数值验证的GPU实现，实现5.1倍应用级加速，在可接受的开发时间内完成；同时检测出5个内核因浮点精度和内建函数差异（包括阈值敏感分支发散和消去效应）导致的数值偏差。

Conclusion: 面向大型遗留科学应用的AI辅助GPU移植，不仅需要代码生成能力，更需要以验证为中心的工作流设计，需管理会话跨越上下文、运行时状态重建以及小规模静态分析遗漏带来的高代价恢复问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have made CLI-based AI agents a practical tool for accelerating GPU porting of large legacy scientific applications. Such applications, however, are not merely old code bases; they are scientific assets whose credibility has been accumulated through long-term development, comparison with observations, and use in domain studies. GPU porting must therefore preserve this scientific validity while adapting the implementation to GPU-centric HPC systems. This paper presents a validation-centric AI-assisted GPU porting workflow through a case study of CReSS, a legacy Fortran weather simulation code with more than 250,000 lines. The workflow uses an AI agent to extract OpenMP regions, generate dump-based kernel benchmarks from physically meaningful simulation states, apply OpenACC transformations, and validate results through element-wise comparison with dumped reference data and application-level validation. Using a real typhoon simulation, the workflow produced numerically validated GPU implementations for 162 target kernels and achieved a 5.1x application-level speedup within practical wall-clock development cost. In particular, it detected numerical discrepancies in five kernels caused by floating-point and intrinsic-function differences, including threshold-sensitive branch divergence and cancellation effects, enabling feedback to the application developers. The case study suggests that, for large legacy scientific applications requiring dump-based validation, practical AI-assisted GPU porting must manage session-spanning context, runtime-state reconstruction, and costly recovery from small static-analysis omissions. These findings demonstrate that AI-assisted GPU porting requires not only code generation, but validation-centric workflow design.

</details>


### [7] [LipCache: A Local Inference Proxy with Certified Caching for Edge Image Classification Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13144)
*Zhengzhe Xiang,Yinlin Chen,Fuli Ying,Binbin Zhou,Hailiang Zhao,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LipCache，一个面向图像分类的认证语义缓存框架。它在不修改部署模型MainNet的前提下，引入带Lipschitz约束的轻量网络GuardNet，将输入映射到低维特征空间，并根据局部分类间隔和分类头的谱范数计算每个样本的认证复用半径。运行时仅当查询特征落入认证复用球内才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet，从而将缓存命中从经验阈值测试转变为具有明确理论边界的几何认证决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端视觉服务向低延迟、高吞吐场景扩展，降低视觉模型推理成本而不牺牲可靠性成为核心问题。现有语义缓存方法依赖经验相似度阈值，虽然能提高命中率，但在决策边界附近容易引入静默误分类，缺乏理论保证。

Method: LipCache框架包含两个核心组件：(1) GuardNet，一个轻量网络，将输入映射到低维特征空间并施加Lipschitz约束；(2) 基于局部分类间隔和分类头谱范数计算逐样本认证复用半径。运行时，仅当查询特征落在认证复用球内才复用缓存结果，否则回退到MainNet。此外还提出了增强的GuardNet训练策略以提高Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展中的缓存命中率。

Result: 在CIFAR、Tiny-ImageNet和SVHN等标准图像分类任务上，LipCache实现了最高1.65倍的实测加速，端到端精度损失有限，所有接受的缓存命中均满足GuardNet侧的认证一致性条件。增强训练策略在Tiny-ImageNet多类扩展中大幅提升缓存命中率，同时保持100%的认证一致性。

Conclusion: 逐样本认证复用能够在保持理论一致性的前提下减少主模型回退，为边缘端可靠的缓存辅助推理提供了一条可行路径。该工作将缓存命中从启发式阈值判断转化为有理论边界保障的几何认证决策。

Abstract: As edge-side vision services continue to expand toward low-latency, high-throughput scenarios, reducing the inference cost of vision models without sacrificing reliability has become a central concern. Existing semantic caching methods largely rely on empirical similarity thresholds; while such thresholds improve hit rates, they tend to introduce silent misclassifications near decision boundaries. To address this, we propose \texttt{LipCache}, a certified semantic caching framework for image classification. Without modifying the existing deployed main model, \texttt{MainNet}, the framework introduces a lightweight network, \texttt{GuardNet}, that maps inputs into a low-dimensional feature space subject to a Lipschitz constraint. It then computes a per-sample certified reuse radius from the local classification margin and the spectral norm of the classification head. At runtime, a cached result is reused only when the query feature falls inside the certified reuse ball; otherwise, the query falls back to \texttt{MainNet}. Thus, cache hits are transformed from empirical threshold tests into geometric certification decisions with explicit theoretical boundaries. Across standard image classification tasks like CIFAR, Tiny-ImageNet, and SVHN, \texttt{LipCache} achieves a measured speedup of up to $1.65\times$ with limited end-to-end accuracy degradation, while all accepted cache hits satisfy the \texttt{GuardNet}-side certified-consistency condition. Furthermore, an enhanced \texttt{GuardNet} training recipe substantially improves cache hit rates in the Tiny-ImageNet multi-class extension while maintaining a certified-consistency rate of $100\%$. These results demonstrate that per-sample certified reuse can reduce main-model fallback while preserving theoretical consistency, providing a feasible approach to reliable cache-assisted inference at the edge.

</details>


### [8] [Triangle-Free Coloring in LOCAL via Resilient Lovász Local Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13357)
*Peter Davies-Peck,Xusheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文改进了Pettie和Su的分布式无三角形图着色算法，利用Davies的"弹性"定义，使LLL实例能在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内求解，获得了第一个用$o(\Delta)$种颜色在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内为无三角形图着色的算法，并推广到围长为5的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式LLL是子对数复杂度算法中的瓶颈问题，尽管已有深入研究，但最快的算法仍需$O(\log_\Delta n)$或$O(\Delta/\log\Delta)$轮。三角形无关着色作为一个基准问题，其现有最优算法依赖多次LLL应用，因此消除LLL瓶颈对改进分布式算法复杂度至关重要。

Method: 文章改编了Pettie-Su算法，采用Davies [SODA 2023]提出的"弹性"（resilience）定义来构造LLL实例，使得这些实例可以在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内求解。当$k=\log^{\omega(1)}\log n$时甚至不需要使用LLL，从而将算法的LLL步骤从瓶颈中解放出来。

Result: 获得了$O(k)+\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮复杂度的无三角形图着色算法，首次实现在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内用$o(\Delta)$种颜色为无三角形图着色。同时给出围长至少为5的图用$(1+\varepsilon)\Delta/\ln\Delta$种颜色的算法，与已知存在性上界匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了分布式LLL在三角形无关着色问题中的瓶颈地位，证明了利用弹性定义可以显著简化LLL实例的求解，开辟了在$\log^{O(1)}\log n$轮内解决此类子对数复杂度问题的途径。

Abstract: The Lovász Local Lemma (LLL) is a probabilistic tool that has been shown to be of central importance in the study of distributed algorithms. For example, the constructive LLL is known to be complete for the class of locally-checkable labeling problems with $o(\log n)$ randomized complexities in the LOCAL model. One classic application of the LLL is in coloring graphs with some sparse structure, such as triangle-free graphs. Triangle-free coloring therefore serves as a benchmark problem for techniques for sublogarithmic randomized distributed algorithms. The state-of-the-art distributed triangle-free coloring algorithm of Pettie and Su [ICALP 2013, Information and Computation 2015] uses $\fracΔ{k}$ colors (where $k$ can be up to $(\frac14 - \varepsilon)\ln Δ$) and consists of $O(k+\log^* n)$ applications of the distributed LLL. However, the distributed LLL is itself a difficult problem; despite significant study, the fastest algorithms known require $O(\log_Δn)$ or $O(\fracΔ{\logΔ})+\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds. In this work, we adapt the Pettie-Su's algorithm so that the resulting LLL instances can be solved in $\log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, by employing the 'resilience' definition of Davies [SODA 2023]. This gives an $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ complexity (since the LLL is not needed when $k= \log^{ω(1)}\log n$), essentially causing the LLL steps to no longer be the bottleneck of the algorithm. As a corollary we obtain the first $\log^{O(1)}\log n$-round algorithms for coloring triangle-free graphs with $o(Δ)$ colors. The same framework also yields a companion girth-$5$ algorithm, using $(1+\varepsilon)Δ/\ln Δ$ colors in $O(k)+ \log^{O(1)}\log n$ rounds, matching the best known existential upper bound for the number of colors.

</details>


### [9] [Fast Tendermint: Speeding Up a Foundational Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13434)
*Preston Vander Vos,Daniel Cason*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Fast Tendermint，将Tendermint共识协议适配到n > 5f场景，在正常情况（good case）下仅需两个通信步骤即可达成决策，同时保留Tendermint的领导者轮换机制，并提供了形式化规范与模型检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链领域对更低延迟的追求推动了对两通信步骤共识协议的探索。现有Tendermint虽然被广泛研究和部署，但需要三个通信步骤才能决策，而最新研究表明在f < n/5条件下共识可以在两个通信步骤内解决。

Method: Fast Tendermint将Tendermint的prevote和precommit步骤合并为单一投票步骤，并将locked和valid状态合并，从而将决策步骤从三个压缩到两个。作者使用Quint（TLA+的现代语法表层）编写了形式化规范，用于对协议进行模型检验。

Result: 作者证明了Fast Tendermint满足一致性（agreement）、有效性（validity）和终止性（termination），并通过Quint形式化规范进行了模型检验验证。

Conclusion: Fast Tendermint在n > 5f条件下实现了两通信步骤的快速共识，同时保持了Tendermint原有的领导者轮换结构，为低延迟区块链提供了可行的共识方案。

Abstract: Tendermint is among the most widely studied and deployed Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, owing in part to its native leader-rotation mechanism that subsumes complex view changes. Like most partially-synchronous BFT protocols, Tendermint tolerates $f < n/3$ Byzantine processes and decides in three communication steps. Motivated by the push for lower-latency blockchains, a recent line of work shows that consensus can be solved in two communication steps when $f < n/5$. We present Fast Tendermint, an adaptation of Tendermint to the $n > 5f$ setting that decides in two communication steps in the good case, while preserving Tendermint's leader-rotation structure. Fast Tendermint collapses Tendermint's prevote and precommit steps into a single voting step and merges the $locked$ and $valid$ state. We give proofs of agreement, validity, and termination, and a formal specification in Quint, a modern surface syntax for TLA+, used to model-check the protocol.

</details>


### [10] [OpScale: Operator-level Provisioning and Autoscaling for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13499)
*Xingqi Cui,Chieh-Jan Mike Liang,Ziang Tang,Jiarong Xing,Haoran Qiu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出OpScale，一个以算子（operator）为粒度进行弹性伸缩的LLM推理服务编排框架，解决了传统以整个模型为缩放单元导致的SLO违例和GPU利用率低下的问题。在最多40块A100和24块GB200的生产环境测试中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下减少了最多36.3%的GPU和28%的功耗，或在固定成本下提升了44%的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务的自动伸缩通常以整个模型为缩放单元，粒度粗、无法捕捉推理工作负载的细粒度动态变化，导致在突发流量下出现SLO违例或GPU资源严重浪费。作者通过特征分析发现算子层面存在显著的异构性，提出以算子为弹性伸缩的基本单元。

Method: OpScale包含四个关键组件：算子级性能剖析（profiling）、资源供给（provisioning）、算子放置（placement）和运行时服务（runtime serving）。针对算子粒度操作带来的高复杂度和搜索空间爆炸问题，OpScale设计了相应的降复杂度和空间剪枝策略。

Result: 在最多40块A100和24块GB200的生产环境轨迹测试中，OpScale在满足SLO的前提下最多可减少36.3%的GPU使用量和28%的功耗；在固定成本预算下可提升44%的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 算子级弹性伸缩是比模型级伸缩更有效的LLM推理资源管理范式，OpScale证明了在算子粒度上实现高效、实用的集群编排是可行且收益显著的。

Abstract: Achieving cost efficiency while meeting strict user-facing SLOs (e.g., time-to-first-token) remains a fundamental challenge for cloud GPU clusters serving large language models (LLMs). Autoscaling is the key mechanism for cluster resource management, yet a basic system design question is open for serving LLMs: what should be the unit of scaling? Existing approaches primarily treat the entire model as a monolithic scaling unit--simple but unable to capture the fine-grained dynamics of inference workloads. As a result, such coarse-grained scaling often leads to either SLO violations under bursty demand or significant GPU under-utilization.
  Our characterization reveals substantial operator heterogeneity, exposing operator-level elasticity as a viable scaling primitive. We present OpScale, a practical operator-level orchestration framework of profiling, provisioning, placement, and runtime serving. OpScale is designed to tackle the high complexity and the space explosion problem, arising from operating at this finer granularity. Evaluated with production traces on up to 40 A100s and 24 GB200s, OpScale attains SLOs with up to 36.3% fewer GPUs and 28% less power, or achieves 44% higher throughput under fixed cost budgets.

</details>

### [11] [MASCOT: Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12532)
*Aaryan Sharma,Vishak Prasad C,Virendra Singh,Ganesh Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出MASCOT方法，通过将多属性多样性形式化为资源分配问题而非流形排斥，解决视觉语言模型在复合属性（如地理和时间）检索中的结果多样化（RD）挑战，在多样性下降任务上显著优于MS-DPP基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型擅长语义相关检索，但在实际应用中仅靠相关性不够，还需要跨复合属性（如地理、时间）的结果多样化控制。现有基于流形排斥的MS-DPP方法在多样性下降任务中面临早排召回率显著下降的问题。

Method: MASCOT将多属性多样化建模为资源分配问题，将属性投影到软分箱空间，并用查询驱动的权重对不同属性进行加权，替代传统的流形排斥策略。

Result: 在PixelProse的三个多样性下降任务上，MASCOT保持88.58%的R@10召回率，而MS-DPP仅67.63%。在复合约束PP_geo_hour任务上，MS-DPP的召回从0.9737骤降至0.4931，R@1仅0.23，而MASCOT保持R@10=0.9410和R@1=0.7202。

Conclusion: MASCOT在复合约束下早期排名召回方面具有显著优势，但作者诚实指出其并非全面优于简单消融方法，优势主要体现在排名1之后以及复合约束场景下的召回保持能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are highly effective in retrieving semantically relevant images. However, in practice, relevance alone is often insufficient. Systems must also achieve Result Diversification (RD) across composite attributes such as geography and time, a task for which precise control remains challenging. Current re-ranking methods, such as Multi-Source Determinantal Point Processes (MS-DPP), address this using manifold-based repulsion over similarity representations. Although this strategy is effective for broad exploration, it exposes a key limitation in manifold-based models: when subjected to diversity-decrease tasks on discrete metadata, they suffer substantial degradation in early-rank recall.
  To bridge this gap, we introduce MASCOT (Model-Aware Submodular Coverage for Composite-Attribute Text-to-Image Retrieval). Instead of relying on manifold repulsion, MASCOT formulates multi-attribute diversity as a resource allocation problem, projecting attributes into a soft-binning space weighted by query-driven importance. Averaged across the three PixelProse diversity-decrease tasks, MASCOT preserves an early-rank recall (R@10) of 88.58%, while MS-DPP retains 67.63%. The margin widens under composite constraints: on PP_geo_hour, where temporal and geographic diversity must be suppressed simultaneously, MS-DPP's recall collapses from 0.9737 to 0.4931 and its top-ranked result degrades to R@1 = 0.23, while MASCOT holds R@10 = 0.9410 and R@1 = 0.7202 at a diversity metric above the unconstrained baseline. We do not claim uniform superiority: on aggregate diversity-relevance scores our own simpler ablations attain higher harmonic means on all three decrease tasks, and MASCOT's advantage is specific to recall beyond rank 1 under composite constraints.

</details>

### [12] [StreamReason-Bench: Can Large Language Models Reason about Event-Time Stream-Processing Semantics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12348)
*Zhuoxi Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了StreamReason-Bench基准测试，用于评估大语言模型对事件时间流处理语义的理解能力。测试覆盖600个生成项目（含滑动、跳跃、会话和处理时间窗口），结果显示模型在事件时间任务上表现不佳，只有默认推理的前沿模型接近解决（0.85），而链式思维可将部分模型准确率从0.34提升至0.48。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式系统越来越多地将工作交给大语言模型（如编写管道、分类警报、读取日志），但这些任务都假设模型理解事件时间流处理的行为方式。作者希望直接检验这一假设，即评估LLM是否真正掌握事件时间流处理的语义。

Method: 构建StreamReason-Bench基准：让模型扮演事件时间流处理器，给定窗口化查询和非有序事件流，报告哪些窗口触发（含聚合值）以及哪些事件被丢弃为迟到事件。答案通过小型Dataflow模型语义参考实现生成，使用精确匹配和部分学分row-F1进行评分。涵盖滑动、跳跃、会话和处理时间四类窗口，并设置处理时间对照组。

Result: 在600个生成项目上，模型在事件时间任务上表现差。直接回答时，遵循指令的模型最高仅达34%精确匹配；链式思维使多个模型准确率翻倍（GPT-4o从0.34升至0.48）；仅有一个默认推理的前沿模型接近解决（0.85）。处理时间对照组几乎所有模型都能解决。错误分析显示迟到数据处理主导事件时间窗口错误，会话窗口主要失败在会话边界确定上。

Conclusion: 事件时间和迟到数据处理是LLM流处理理解中的核心难点，而非窗口化或算术运算。该基准为模型在流处理语义上的能力提供了可精确评分的量化评估，揭示了当前模型在这类关键任务中的明显短板。

Abstract: Streaming systems increasingly hand work to large language models (LLMs) -- writing pipelines, triaging alerts, reading logs -- and all of it assumes the model knows how event-time stream processing behaves. We test that assumption head-on. StreamReason-Bench asks a model to stand in for an event-time stream processor: given a windowed query and a stream of out-of-order events, it has to report which windows fire (with their aggregates) and which events are dropped as late. The answer key comes from a small reference implementation of Dataflow-model semantics, so we can grade exactly, and with a partial-credit row-F1, without running an engine. On 600 generated items covering tumbling, hopping, session, and processing-time windows, the models do poorly on event time. Told to answer directly, no model that actually follows the instruction clears 34% exact match; chain-of-thought (CoT) roughly doubles that for several of them (GPT-4o goes from 0.34 to 0.48), and only one frontier model that reasons by default comes near solving the set (0.85). A processing-time control, with no watermarks and nothing late, is almost solved by every capable model. That gap points to event-time and late-data handling, not windowing or arithmetic, as the hard part. Sorting errors by window type tells the same story: late-data mistakes dominate the event-time windows and vanish on the control, and session windows mostly fail on where the session boundaries fall.

</details>


### [13] [FluctlightDB: A Memory Model of Data for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12365)
*Ganesh S*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将长期智能体记忆视为一种独立的数据模型，而非传统关系模型或向量模型的扩展。作者介绍了FluctlightDB——一个嵌入式引擎，通过experience()和activate()实现该数据契约，包含专属的读写语义（编码、分离、巩固、溯源，以及线索驱动的记忆图激活）。论文在多组基准测试中报告了高召回率（LoCoMo 99.0%，LongMemEval-S 97.6%），并表明这并非对现有记忆层的创新替代，而是其下方的嵌入式引擎契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去五十年，数据系统只回答两种问题：关系模型的谓词匹配和向量模型的近邻查询。两者都不是为跨长会话的线索驱动、溯源加权的回忆而设计的。作者认为长时智能体记忆需要一种截然不同的数据模型，而现有系统（如Mem0、Zep、HippoRAG）在数据栈中缺少一个底层嵌入式引擎契约。

Method: 提出FluctlightDB嵌入式引擎，实现长期记忆数据模型：写语义包括编码（encoding）、分离（separation）、巩固（consolidation）和溯源（provenance）；读语义通过链接记忆图实现线索驱动的激活（cue-driven activation）。提供experience()和activate()两个核心API，并附带CHORUS/PRISM检索组件。支持通过pip安装编译后的wheel包快速验证。

Result: 在LoCoMo官方证据召回指标上（10段对话，1,982个黄金片段），CHORUS召回率达99.0%（2026年7月内部复现）。在LongMemEval-S（500题，官方session_recall@8）上，检索管线达97.6%（488/500），端到端QA达97.4%（487/500）。在BEIR SciFact上与Chroma比较，nDCG@10为0.646 vs 0.645，Recall@10为0.792 vs 0.783。作者自建小回归套件（FAMB）宏平均100%。

Conclusion: 作者并不主张超越了Mem0、Zep或HippoRAG等记忆层，而是提出在其下方新增一个被缺失的数据栈层次——一个嵌入式引擎契约。作者不宣称提出新的神经科学或新的Transformer架构，而是交付一个可复现、可质疑的引擎实现（MIT许可），主张这代表数据系统领域一个新的、独立的存储/检索范式。

Abstract: For fifty years, data systems have answered two questions. The relational model asked which records match a predicate; the vector model asked which vectors lie nearest a query. Neither was built for cue-driven, provenance-weighted recall across long sessions. We propose treating long-term agent memory as a distinct data model -- with its own write semantics (encoding, separation, consolidation, provenance) and read semantics (cue-driven activation across a linked memory graph) -- and present FluctlightDB, an embedded engine that implements this contract via experience() and activate(). We make that case carefully, not categorically: we do not claim novelty over Mem0, Zep, or HippoRAG-style memory layers, only an embedded engine contract beneath them. On LoCoMo (official evidence-recall metric; 10 conversations, 1,982 gold spans), CHORUS recalls 99.0% on an internally reproduced July 2026 run. On LongMemEval-S (500 questions, official session_recall@8), our retrieval harness scores 97.6% (488/500); end-to-end QA with our reader/judge stack scores 97.4% (487/500) -- these layers use different protocols than vendor leaderboard figures we cite for context only. On BEIR SciFact (shared MiniLM embeddings, same harness, Recall Fabric on), CHORUS/PRISM edges Chroma on nDCG@10 (0.646 vs. 0.645) and Recall@10 (0.792 vs. 0.783). We also report a small author-designed regression suite (FAMB; paraphrase n=10, other sub-tests n=1) at 100% macro -- internal validation, not peer benchmark. Strangers can verify the engine in under a minute via pip install "fluctlightdb[native]" and a minimal connect() -> experience() -> activate() script (compiled wheel, not source-only). Harnesses and frozen JSON are MIT-licensed. We claim no new neuroscience and no new transformer; we propose a missing layer of the data stack and release an engine others can reproduce and contest.

</details>


### [14] [Lifecycle-Aware Archival for Asymmetric Financial Datasets: A Production Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12367)
*Tulika Manek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Razorpay在PostgreSQL Aurora上为金融交易服务设计并部署的一套生命周期感知的归档系统，通过分析"名人分区问题"并提出基于ID单调性的去重技术，实现了95%热存储缩减、53%基础设施成本下降和约60%的p99延迟改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型金融交易数据库面临运营新鲜度与存储效率之间的根本矛盾。随着数据量增长（数十TB、数十亿记录、数千TPS峰值写入），需要在不改变Schema的前提下解决归档系统中热数据与冷数据分离带来的性能问题。

Method: 1) 分析刻画"名人分区问题"：生命周期状态分区将所有活跃行集中在默认分区，导致O(N×M)规划开销、O(N)执行I/O回退和状态转换时的写放大。2) 提出基于ID单调性的去重技术：利用Snowflake IDs和ULIDs等单调递增ID中的时间编码，仅将可能已归档的重复插入路由到温数据库查找，无需布隆过滤器、幂等表或外部依赖。

Result: 生产环境全量部署结果：热存储缩减95%，月度数据库基础设施成本降低53%，服务级p99处理延迟降低约60%，写进程CPU利用率降低51个百分点，且在峰值写入TPS下持续稳定运行，主交易表无需Schema变更。

Conclusion: 该生命周期感知归档系统证明了在不修改主表Schema的前提下，通过巧妙的ID语义利用和分区策略优化，可以同时实现显著的成本节约和性能提升，为大规模金融交易数据库的冷热数据分离提供了可复用的生产级方案。

Abstract: Large-scale financial transaction databases face a fundamental tension between operational freshness requirements and storage efficiency. We present the design, implementation, and production evaluation of a lifecycle-aware archival system for a financial transaction service at Razorpay managing billions of records on PostgreSQL Aurora (version 14), occupying tens of terabytes of storage and sustaining peak write throughput in the thousands of TPS. We make two contributions. First, we analytically characterize the Celebrity Partition Problem: lifecycle-state partitioning concentrates all operationally active rows in a single default partition, incurring O(N x M) planning overhead, O(N) execution I/O regression, and write amplification on every state transition. Second, we present an ID-monotonicity-based deduplication technique that exploits temporal encoding in monotonically increasing ID schemes (Snowflake IDs, ULIDs, and equivalent) to route only potentially-archived re-inserts to a warm database lookup, requiring no Bloom filters, idempotency tables, or external dependencies. We report production results from a fully deployed system: 95% hot storage size reduction, 53% reduction in monthly database infrastructure cost, ~60% reduction in service-level p99 processing latency, 51 percentage-point reduction in writer CPU utilization, and sustained operation at peak write TPS - without schema changes to the primary transaction table.

</details>


### [15] [Pipeline Denotational Design: Correct-by-Construction Data Pipelines at Zero Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12375)
*Nikos Karayannidis*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出管线指称设计（PDD）方法论，在语义域而非代码域中设计数据管线，通过类型化操作代数保证任何良类型组合都在粒度（grain）上正确。正确性在三个层次（粒度、行为类、领域）于设计时建立，零成本且无需数据访问，通过Pipeline Correctness定理将验证归结为单一输入边界检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理大规模生成管线代码，验证成为瓶颈。最关键的错误——粒度不一致导致的聚合结果膨胀——能逃避模式检查、类型检查和采样测试。现有方法无法在设计阶段保证正确性。

Method: 在语义域中设计管线：由类型化操作代数（Pipeline Design Algebra）组合设计，保证良类型组合的粒度正确性。正确性分三层建立——粒度由数据无关计算（CalcG），行为类由类型检查器，领域规则通过带证明的组合。最终通过Pipeline Correctness定理将验证化简为输入边界检查，并生成SQL/PySpark验证查询。

Result: 实现了生产级工具链，提供验证频谱：运行时检查、部署级类型检查器、以及Agda/Lean 4中的机器检查证明。支持跨管线模式、行为类和数据建模范式的评估，且由于仅依赖粒度，可扩展到AI生成的语义层和本体查询。

Conclusion: 正确性构建是一个频谱而非二值属性。该方法重新定义了工程师角色：AI代理实例化预验证模式并生成可机器检查的证书，人类验证紧凑规范。基于粒度单一概念即可实现普适的设计时保证。

Abstract: Pipeline Denotational Design (PDD) is a design-first methodology for building data pipelines that are correct by construction. As AI agents generate pipeline code at scale, the bottleneck shifts from writing to verifying pipelines, and the errors that matter most (grain inconsistencies that silently inflate aggregates) evade schema checks, type checks, and sampled tests. PDD designs pipelines in a semantic domain rather than in code: a design is composed from a typed algebra of operations (the Pipeline Design Algebra, one instantiation) in which every well-typed composition is grain-correct by construction. Resting on grain alone, the guarantee is universal: it holds for any grain-inferring operation set, over any engine. Correctness is established in three layers (grain, behavioral class, and domain) at design time, at zero cost, with no access to data: grain by a data-independent computation (CalcG), behavioral class by the type checker, and domain rules by a proof-carrying composition over operation contracts. A Pipeline Correctness theorem collapses the rest to a single input-boundary check: the only data-dependent residue is whether inputs meet the design's preconditions (data quality, not code correctness), which PDD emits as SQL/PySpark verification queries. Correctness by construction is a spectrum: the same design can be verified by runtime checks, the deployed type-level checker, or machine-checked proofs in Agda/Lean 4. This recasts the engineer's role: an agent instantiates a pre-verified pattern and ships a machine-checkable certificate; the human validates a compact specification and checks it. We realize the methodology in a production toolchain and set out an evaluation across pipeline patterns, behavioral classes, and data modelling paradigms. Because it rests on grain alone, the same design-time check extends to AI-generated queries over semantic layers and ontologies.

</details>

*Malcolm Crowe,Fritz Laux*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种结合传统关系数据模型与图模型的数据管理服务器，通过将外键替换为引用值（基于日志文件中记录地址）来统一两种模型，从而提升性能，支持高效的链接追踪而非查找连接，并展示了基于三元组的知识库示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据库的关系模型与新兴数据产品的图模型之间存在差异，传统外键连接（join）性能低下，无法有效支持大语言模型等生成工具所需的知识建模与链接追踪需求。

Method: 在实现层面将两种模型进行调和，用引用值（reference values）替代传统外键。引用值基于日志文件中的记录地址生成，永不改变，从而在保留关系模型数据组织的同时，实现了图模型中的链接直接追踪能力，避免了查找连接的开销。

Result: 该方法提升了数据访问性能，使系统能够有效支持链接追踪而非查找连接，并且验证了基于三元组的知识库（例如RDF式表示）也能在该系统中得到支持。

Conclusion: 这种关系模型与图模型的统一数据管理服务器为大型语言模型的生成工具提供了知识模型支持，证明了在单一系统中同时容纳关系数据与图数据的可行性。

Abstract: A data management server that combines the relational data model found in traditional databases with the graphical models being developed by newer data products can provide its knowledge model to generative tools for large language models. Both models have been reconciled at the implementation level by replacing foreign keys by reference values. This boosts performance and allows effectively following links instead of lookup joins. The reference value will never change as it uses the record address in the log file. This paper reports on progress in providing such a synthesis and includes an example of a knowledge base that is triple-based and would also be supported in such a system.

</details>

*Emanuele Cavalleri,Paolo Perlasca,J. Harry Caufield,Justin Reese,Christopher J. Mungall,Marco Mesiti*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了SchemaLink，一个用于图形化构建和增强LinkML模式的Web环境，通过图形化语言、规范化规范和基于RAG的AI辅助编辑功能，帮助非专业生物策展人更高效地开发和维护LinkML模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkML虽然已应用于多个生物医学场景，但开发与维护其模式对新手策展人具有挑战性，非专家可能难以掌握LinkML语法和最佳实践，需要大量时间与精力才能构建结构良好的模式。

Method: SchemaLink通过三个关键途径解决上述问题：引入图形化语言用于LinkML模式的可视化规范；统一相似上下文中的模式规范；利用基于RAG（检索增强生成）的方法简化设计与策展流程，辅助策展人从零创建新模式及编辑已有模式。

Conclusion: SchemaLink作为一个开源可用的Web环境，为LinkML模式的图形化构建与AI辅助编辑提供了完整解决方案，降低了非专家策展人使用LinkML的门槛。

Abstract: Motivation: LinkML is a suitable language for the representation of the structural and content constraints of different kinds of biomedical data. Even if it is a quite recent proposal, it has been applied in several biomedical contexts. Developing and maintaining LinkML schemas presents several challenges, particularly for novice curators. Non-expert bio-curators may struggle with LinkML syntax and best practices, requiring significant time and effort to develop well-structured schemas.
  Results: In this paper we propose SchemaLink, a web-based environment for the graphical construction and enhancement of LinkML schemas that address the following requirements: $(i)$ introduce a graphical language for the specification of LinkML schemas, $(ii)$ make uniform the specification of schemas in similar contexts, $(iii)$ simplify the design and curation processes by exploiting a RAG-based approach to assist curators in creating new schemas from scratch and editing already developed ones. Several experimental analyses show the quality of the produced LinkML schemas through the AI-based editing facilities.
  Availability and Implementation: SchemaLink is available online at: https://SchemaLink.biodata.di.unimi.it. SchemaLink code and testing data are available as open-source on GitHub at: https://github.com/AnacletoLAB/{schemalink-webapp,schemalink-api}.

</details>


### [18] [ASAP: Reimagining the Data Lifecycle using Application Semantic-Aware Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12735)
*Milind Srivastava,Zeying Zhu,Yajie Zhou,Yancheng Yuan,Fenghao Dong,Peilin Xin,Zaoxing Liu,Vyas Sekar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAP（应用语义感知处理）范式，主张将跨领域已有的语义感知优化原语（如近似查询处理、草图、压缩、汇总）统一为数据管道中的第一类设计原则，以系统性地应对CSP问题（低成本、大规模、高性能的矛盾）。初步证据表明可带来高达3个数量级的CSP权衡改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据采集-传输-存储-分析的整个生命周期中，许多领域（可观测性、网络、安全等）都面临CSP问题（在低成本和大规模下保持高性能）。现有方案（如数据库中的AQP、网络路由器中的草图等）各自在生命周期某阶段孤立地解决问题，缺乏统一的视角，错失了跨生命周期协同优化的机会。

Method: 提出ASAP范式，将"应用语义感知处理"确立为数据管道的第一类设计原则。其核心主张是：(i)对不同领域中开发的各种ASAP原语进行统一视角审视；(ii)实现跨生命周期优化，如直接对源头计算的草图进行分析；(iii)借鉴其他应用领域的原语，推动这些技术的广泛采用。

Result: 初步实验证据表明，采用ASAP范式可在许多应用领域中实现CSP权衡上高达3个数量级的改进。

Conclusion: ASAP范式有望通过统一各领域的语义感知原语、贯穿数据全生命周期进行协同优化，从而系统性解决CSP问题；但尚需进一步研究如何将这些技术的收益社会化推广，以促使其被广泛采纳。

Abstract: Across many domains (e.g., observability, networking, security), data processing pipelines face what we refer to as the CSP problem: achieving low Cost at large Scale, while maintaining high Performance. In response, we see several efforts to tackle CSP in various stages of the Collect-Transmit-Store-Analyze data lifecycle; such as approximate query processing in databases or sketches in network routers. Our work is driven by the simple insight: "seeing the forest for the trees". These proposed solutions (e.g., AQP, sketching, compression, rollups) addressing CSP share a common property - they exploit semantic-preserving opportunities to support application needs. In this paper, we make a case for ASAP, a paradigm that makes Application Semantic-Aware Processing (ASAP) a first-class design principle in data processing pipelines. We argue that by taking a unified view across ASAP primitives developed in different domains, across the entire data lifecycle, we can unlock new opportunities to tackle the CSP problem. In particular, we can: (i) enable novel cross-lifecycle optimizations such as analytics run directly on sketches computed at the source; (ii) leverage primitives developed in other application domains; and (iii) enable widespread adoption of these powerful techniques. We discuss research challenges in socializing the benefits of the ASAP paradigm, and show preliminary evidence that adopting ASAP can yield up to 3 orders of magnitude improvements in the CSP tradeoff for many application domains.

</details>


### [19] [Semantic Intelligence Against CSAM: The PreventCSA@EU Ontology Framework for Classification and Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12979)
*Elias Tzortzakakis,Emmanouela Kokolaki,Evangelia Daskalaki,Paraskevi Fragopoulou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了PreventCSA@EU本体，一个语义化的框架，用于支持在线儿童性虐待和性剥削材料（CSAM/CSEM）的识别、分类、标注和分析。该本体通过系统综述和比较分析现有相关本体与分类体系构建，围绕核心实体（如媒体对象、内容、人员、描绘和调查报告）建立层次化语义模型，并与INHOPE UCS标签、Dublin Core和Schema.org等标准对齐，旨在支持跨国执法机构间的协作和自动化处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSAM/CSEM在数字环境中的日益传播，加上各国在法律定义和分类实践上的不一致性，凸显了对语义互操作框架的需求，以支持跨组织合作和自动化处理。现有分类方法缺乏统一的语义基础，难以支撑欧盟层面的数据基础设施和执法协作。

Method: 通过系统综述和比较分析现有的CSA/CSE相关、元数据导向及调查性本体与分类体系，设计了一个围绕核心实体（Media Object、Content、Person、Depiction、Investigative Report）的层次化语义模型，并与INHOPE UCS、Dublin Core-DMCI和Schema.org等既有标准进行结构化对齐。

Result: 提出了PreventCSA@EU本体框架，该框架通过本体驱动的互操作性实现CSA/CSE相关数据的结构化标注和分析，支持一致的分类、儿童识别和调查流程，并增强了跨系统兼容性，对欧盟中心数据库（CSAR下的拟议欧盟中心数据库）具有特别相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过整合既定分类标准与新型层次化本体，扩展了现有分类方法，增加了数据库概念化、过程建模和本体驱动数据管理的概念结构，为欧盟层面的数据基础设施提供了语义互操作的解决方案。

Abstract: This work presents the PreventCSA@EU ontology, a semantically grounded framework designed to support the identification, classification, annotation, and analysis of online Child Sexual Abuse and Child Sexual Exploitation Material (CSAM/CSEM). The growing circulation and dissemination of CSAM/CSEM across digital environments, combined with inconsistencies in legal definitions and classification practices across jurisdictions, highlights the need for semantically interoperable frameworks capable of supporting cross-organizational cooperation and automated processing. The proposed ontology is developed through a systematic review and comparative analysis of existing CSA/CSE-related, metadata oriented, and investigative ontologies and taxonomies, with its primary design aimed at addressing the operational needs and domain-specific requirements of national LEA Directorates. It introduces a hierarchical semantic model built around core entities such as Media Object, Content, Person, Depiction, and Investigative Report, while enabling structured alignment with INHOPE UCS labels, Dublin Core-DMCI Metadata Terms, and Schema.org. The proposed framework emphasizes ontology-driven interoperability for structured annotation and analysis of CSA/CSE-related data, supporting consistent classification, child identification, and investigative processes for offender prosecution. The design aims extend existing classification approaches with additional conceptual structures for database conceptualization, process modeling, and ontology-driven data management. By integrating established classification standards with a novel hierarchical ontology, the proposed framework enhances cross-system compatibility, with particular relevance to emerging EU-level data infrastructures, including the envisaged EU Center database under the proposed Child Sexual Abuse Regulation (CSAR).

</details>

### [20] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoKiFormer，一种新型LLM架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和MoE中知识存储与计算路径耦合的问题，实现预训练收敛速度提升1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两个效率瓶颈：自注意力缺乏局部建模的显式归纳偏置，导致序列内部局部信息被冗余建模；MoE隐式耦合知识存储与计算路径，阻碍了序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。这两个局限性限制了LLM的训练效率和知识利用能力。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准decoder架构上增加两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），在注意力机制中引入卷积融合，显式捕获局部模式并使注意力操作在更具信息量的表示上；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，将存储与计算解耦，支持直接的知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练阶段的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在信息整合效率方面的优越性。

Conclusion: 通过显式解决局部与全局两个层面上的信息处理瓶颈，LoKiFormer展示了超越现有LLM架构的效率优势，为更高效的大模型预训练提供了一种可行的架构设计思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [21] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量和卫星天气），针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅的冰湖溃决洪水、降雨引发的滑坡和冰川池塘洪水三类灾害，构建并评估了灾害易感性与触发时机的预测模型。结果显示：前兆天气在触发时机方面表现良好（ROC 0.73/0.83/0.82），而地形在易感性排序上表现一般（0.76/0.71/0.54），深度学习方法未显著优于简单梯度提升基线，最终提出了尼泊尔监测优先清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有可行性研究指出变形信号可标识哪些湖正在失稳、天气可标识何时有风险，但缺乏可操作的预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用完全免费的数据构建能够预测"哪些地点易感"和"何时触发"的模型。

Method: 使用589个已定年溃决事件（HMAGLOFDB）与数千条滑坡编目数据，将每个事件与相似但未失稳的站点配对；采用空间交叉验证（整幅地图瓦片预留）防止邻域记忆效应；分别针对三类灾害建立前兆天气触发模型和地形易感性模型，并与强简单基线对比；测试五种深度学习模型及梯度提升基线和三规则决策树。

Result: 前兆天气定时触发效果：大溃决ROC 0.73，滑坡0.83，小洪水0.82；地形排序易感性经可信匹配后为0.76/0.71/0.54（后者与随机无异）；溃决信号在单一区域内增强，尼泊尔单独达0.89；深度学习模型未显著优于基线，其中三类随机梯度提升略高但不足以确认，湖泊灾害中基线胜出；最终产出尼泊尔分级监测清单。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据可用于"何时"的预测，但对"哪里"的易感排序仅在部分地区有效；研究提供了监测优先排序工具而非绝对预测，并明确了免费数据的适用边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [22] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH（Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History）架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，并为每个锚点关联内容条件锚点键，使状态空间模型在保持计算效率的同时突破固定尺寸状态限制，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer凭借随上下文长度增长的token级记忆实现强大的长上下文检索能力，但训练时需承受二次方计算复杂度，推理时键值缓存也线性增长。循环替代方案虽能通过固定尺寸状态高效解码，但早期关联常被后续更新覆盖，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。现有方法难以在计算效率与长范围记忆保留之间取得良好平衡。

Method: MARCH周期性地缓存累积的循环状态检查点作为状态锚点，并为每个锚点生成紧凑的内容条件锚点键。它维护一个可随上下文长度增长的记忆库，提供历史分辨率与记忆成本之间的可控权衡。在生成每个token时，MARCH生成锚点查询以关注所有因果可用的状态锚点，输出通过对所有历史锚点进行注意力式聚合并结合当前状态计算得到。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环模型的长范围记忆能力，同时保留其原生计算路径的高效性。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容路由的状态锚点缓存机制，成功将状态空间模型扩展到固定尺寸之外，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了长上下文召回性能，为循环架构在长序列任务中提供了有竞争力的替代方案。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [23] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于价值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法VGG-MADiffRL及其层次化控制架构MDCA，用于多AUV自组网协同目标跟踪，在声学通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动等复杂水下环境下实现更稳定、更精确的协同跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体强化学习的多AUV协同跟踪方法存在高维联合状态-动作建模困难、策略生成对噪声敏感等问题，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化，亟需一种更稳健的学习算法来应对水下复杂环境的挑战。

Method: 提出了VGG-MADiffRL算法和MDCA三层闭环控制架构。MDCA包含全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层和物理动作执行层，实现任务分配、本地决策与执行反馈的协同优化。VGG-MADiffRL基于扩散策略，在反向去噪过程中引入价值梯度引导动作生成，并采用孪生价值网络配合联合优化和软目标更新，抑制过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中实现了更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL结合扩散策略和价值梯度引导，有效解决了多AUV协同跟踪中高维建模和噪声敏感问题，为受限通信条件下的水下自主协同提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [24] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将生成式建模统一表述为路径积分形式，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均作为同一主作用量的不同评估原则出现。利用MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，引入图解微扰论，并给出单圈修正，在无需额外随机采样成本的情况下将确定性采样器的误差从53%降至1.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成建模方法（流模型、扩散模型、变分模型、对抗模型）各自独立发展，缺乏统一的数学框架来系统分析其误差来源与改进方向，也难以从第一性原理出发对采样过程进行修正。

Method: 将生成建模表述为路径积分，以马丁-西格里亚-罗斯-扬森-德多米尼西斯（MSRJD）形式分离自由与相互作用概率流，引入费曼图式微扰展开。单圈修正以解析形式加入确定性采样器，无需额外随机采样；不完美学习分数作为插入项引入响应加权得分匹配目标，对称等变漂移设计成为具有EFT功率计数的算符展开。

Result: 在可解和非线性漂移上验证了单圈修正的有效性，将树级误差从53%降至1.6%，显著提升了采样精度且不增加随机采样成本。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成建模提供了一个统一的物理语言，使得不同生成模型可以共享微扰论工具，并可将统计误差校正、得分匹配目标改进和对称性设计系统地纳入同一理论体系内。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [25] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，这是为动态图边级异常检测框架AddGraph设计的首个严格事后可解释性框架。通过双时空归因（DSTA）机制，在不改变检测性能（Delta AUC=0）的前提下，为异常边提供空间、短期时序和长期时序三个维度的解释，并在UCI Message基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习动态图异常检测器虽然准确率很高，但缺乏可解释性——当边被标记为异常时，分析者只能得到一个分数而得不到任何理由。在协作型、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的，因为自动化决策必须可审计且可信。AddGraph作为基础的GCN+GRU框架从未配备任何形式的可解释性，这正是本文要解决的核心问题。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph，一个严格后置（post-hoc）的可解释性框架，基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件，各自对应AddGraph的一个架构模块：(1) 基于梯度的相关性归因，作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；(2) 直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时序维度，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时序维度）。由于检测器被冻结，检测性能精确保持不变。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线达到每快照平均AUC为0.8705，超过原始发表结果。X-AddGraph精确复现每个分数且新增解释。在四类边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上评估，长期归因识别出的历史快照携带显著更多的反事实信号（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲解释器无法提供的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph提供了零性能损失的完整可解释性方案，证明了事后归因机制可以有效弥补动态图异常检测器的透明度缺陷，且长期时序归因具有独特的反事实解释价值。代码已开源供复现。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [26] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于学习的睡眠分期图谱（LBH）方法，通过建模每位评分者的阶段特定行为（混淆矩阵），将多位专家的评分聚合为更可靠的参考标注。在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上，使用随机森林分类器结合EEG+EMG特征取得了最佳性能（准确率约86%），优于传统的单一参考图谱和最佳评分者图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动睡眠分期研究通常使用单一参考图谱进行评估，忽略了评分者间已知的变异性。作者希望通过利用多元评分数据集，从多位专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标签，以提升自动分期的评估准确性和可靠性。

Method: 使用DOD-H和DOD-O公开数据集，从EEG（C3-M2）和chin EMG信号中提取每30秒epoch的30维特征（共60维）。提出LBH方法：利用机器学习模型的混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的阶段特定行为，经列归一化后估计真实睡眠阶段的条件概率，跨评分者聚合概率分配最终标签。使用随机森林、SVM和MLP分类器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集图谱（DH）和最佳评分者图谱（BSH）对比。

Result: LBH在整体性能上一致优于DH和BSH。最佳结果来自随机森林结合EEG+EMG设置：在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模可以改善参考图谱的构建，且无需丢弃任何单个专家的信息，为利用多元评分数据提升睡眠分期评估可靠性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [27] [A Contract-Grade Verifier for LLM-Generated GPU Kernels, and a Native Blackwell Backward for the Gated-Linear-Recurrence Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12700)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个12道对抗性检验的"契约级"GPU内核验证器，发现公开系统已接受的2,638个机器生成内核中39.5%存在无法用容差解释的严重错误，62.1%至少有一项违规；同时用该验证器评估了首个原生Blackwell tcgen05上的GDN训练反向内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成GPU内核的正确性评估仅依赖"运行几次随机输入并比较输出接近度"的宽松测试，内核可能通过该测试但仍存在静默错误（如NaN/Inf问题、运行不稳定、shape变化时出错、fp16累积误差等），导致正确性信号远弱于报告所暗示的水平。

Method: 设计了一个包含12个对抗性检查门的契约级验证器，每个门是正确内核必须满足的属性，多个门免容差（tolerance-free），使任何阈值选择都无法掩盖错误。对外审计2,638个已被公共系统测试接受的内核；对内验证自研的Blackwell tcgen05 GDN训练反向内核（含反向状态阶段），并用双精度oracle独立验证。

Result: 发现39.5%的内核存在无法靠容差解释的破坏性问题，62.1%至少有一项违规；标准测试接受了1,487个验证器拒绝的内核，反向仅14个。通过四项独立方式捍卫结论：7/7阳性对照、阈值校准扫掠、与参考基准正确性代码98.5%一致、分层人工审计。自研内核通过了验证并成功训练五个family成员。

Conclusion: 内核生成领域报告的正确性信号远弱于数字暗示的水平，容差无关的契约性检查可大幅缩小这一差距；同时展示了首个原生Blackwell tcgen05上的GDN训练反向内核的可行性。

Abstract: Systems that generate GPU kernels with language models report high correctness rates. Those rates come from a single loose test: run the kernel on a few random inputs at one fixed shape and accept it if the output is close to a reference. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. We build the instrument that checks correctness properly: a contract-grade verifier of twelve adversarial gates, each a property a correct kernel must satisfy, several of them tolerance-free, so no choice of threshold can explain a failure away. Aimed outward, the verifier audits 2,638 machine-generated kernels that a public system's own harness had already accepted as correct. It finds 39.5% broken beyond any tolerance argument and 62.1% carrying at least one violation. The field's standard test accepts 1,487 kernels the verifier rejects, against only 14 the other way. We defend the finding four independent ways: a 7/7 positive control, a threshold-calibration sweep, 98.5% agreement with the reference benchmark's own correctness code, and a stratified hand-audit. Aimed inward, the verifier judges a kernel of our own: the first native Blackwell tcgen05 training backward for the gated-linear-recurrence (GDN) family, including the reverse-state stage the field still runs on a fallback. We establish its correctness independently, against a double-precision oracle, and train five family members through it. The correctness signal behind reported progress in kernel generation is far weaker than the numbers suggest, and a set of tolerance-free contracts would close most of the gap.

</details>


### [28] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现预测方向（prediction direction）是Transformer模型中一种内容定义的受特权锚点。残差流中的变异在几何和行为上按与预测方向的邻近度分层：靠近预测方向的窄界面集中了读出相关结构，而远距离的互补区域则随模型规模扩展。这一分层结构在18个模型（7B-120B）中普遍成立，且方差分析只能部分捕捉这一组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer内部表示组织方式，特别是"特权基"（privileged bases）如何选择坐标轴，以及高维计算如何与线性读出共存。此前对坐标轴的分析未能解释预测方向作为特权锚点的作用。

Method: 以预测方向（unembedding方向）作为锚点，测量残差流变异相对于该锚点的几何和行为分层。对18个模型（包括密集和MoE类型，7B-120B，base和instruction-tuned）进行系统性测试，并通过因果干预（破坏不同距离层级的方向）验证各区域的功能角色。

Result: 所有18个模型均显示分层结构：预测近端区域高度结构化、聚集相关提示，预测远端区域更平坦且反区分提示组。破坏最靠近预测方向的方差方向会导致立即发散和任务框架偏移，破坏次层级则延迟发散但保持框架。互补区域虽然每个方向与读出的对齐较弱，但在因果和时间上是承重的。

Conclusion: 预测方向是一个区别于先前描述坐标轴的受特权锚点，其窄界面在功能上具有决定性。该结果提供了高维计算如何与线性读出共存的几何解释，并揭示行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI虽有专家级诊断能力，但在资源受限的农村环境中（带宽稀缺、计算有限、需整合多模态数据）难以普及。现有云中心化方案和纯边缘方案都无法同时兼顾准确性、延迟和成本，需要新的架构设计来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 构建云-边缘协同架构：边缘端部署轻量级领域专用模型，将原始医疗数据转换为紧凑的结构化输出；云端LLM综合这些输出生成临床摘要；另设LLM编排器根据患者上下文动态选择诊断工具，避免处理无关输入，实现全面的模态覆盖。

Result: 在20个多模态临床案例（涵盖心脏、产科、创伤和筛查场景）和三种模拟网络配置（500kbps-5Mbps）下评估：混合系统达到98-99%的诊断工具召回率和92-96%的精确率，临床准确性与纯云端基线相当或更优，延迟保持带宽无关（25-35秒），token成本降低4-15倍。

Conclusion: 架构设计在受限部署条件下实现高效多模态整合和更好的事实推理方面起到关键作用，该云-边缘协同方案为资源匮乏环境中的医疗AI落地提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Medical AI has demonstrated specialist-level diagnostic accuracy, yet these capabilities remain largely inaccessible in resource-constrained rural settings where bandwidth is scarce, compute is limited, and clinical decision-making requires integrating heterogeneous modalities. We introduce a cloud--edge collaborative architecture that addresses these constraints: lightweight, domain-specific models on the edge transform raw medical data into compact structured outputs, while a cloud LLM synthesizes these outputs into clinical summaries. An LLM-based orchestrator dynamically selects diagnostic tools based on patient context, promoting comprehensive modality coverage without processing irrelevant inputs. We evaluate on 20 multimodal clinical cases spanning cardiac, obstetric, trauma, and screening scenarios under three simulated network profiles (500,kbps--5,Mbps). The hybrid system achieves 98--99% diagnostic tool recall with 92--96% precision, matches or exceeds cloud-only baselines on clinical accuracy, and maintains bandwidth-invariant latency (25--35,s) at 4--15x lower token cost. These results highlight the role of architectural design in enabling efficient multimodal integration and improving factual grounding compared to cloud-only approaches under deployment constraints.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过机器学习表征编码大量实验观测数据，再应用摘要程序（summarization procedure）选择最小子集的高度显著示例，以提供基于相似性的客观分类标准。
